ZIP gồm video.mp4, channels/<số>-<platform>/content.json cùng title/caption/hashtags/tags/comment.txt và cover.jpg nếu chọn; manifest.json giữ hash/bytes/revision/draftVersion/snapshotId. Các endpoint GET/POST /api/v1/publish-packs dùng project tường minh, GET có id tải ZIP; GET không id liệt kê final/gói cũ. Chưa cross-process build ownership, queue/Activity/progress byte cho pack, ZIP64/>3GiB, benchmark video lớn hoặc recovery orphan temp do kill giữa ghi. Đóng dialog abort request đóng gói đang theo dõi; mất phản hồi sát lúc commit có thể đã tạo gói, tải lại từ danh sách thay vì khẳng định rollback.

Theo yêu cầu cập nhật tài liệu, BUILD_PLAN, đặc tả dựng song song mục 11–12, đặc tả UI mục 137, kiến trúc engine và AGENTS được đồng bộ. Mỗi máy chạy dự án riêng; NAS chia sẻ nguồn. Không tự mở phiên chat hoặc suy ra phiên sống từ việc đã đăng nhập; reported là báo liên lạc gần đây có kiểm owner. Cùng project vẫn phải kiểm quyền áp dụng/revision.

Mở lại dialog nối job đang chạy trong cùng vòng đời server; đóng dialog không tự hủy. Chặn thêm khi project đổi hoặc còn sửa chưa lưu, chờ editor chuẩn bị xong trước tạo cảnh.

## Cập nhật tài liệu dựng nhiều video · 04/11/2026

Theo yêu cầu “update tài liệu”, đã đồng bộ BUILD_PLAN, đặc tả dựng song song mục 8–10, rà soát UX mục 12 và AGENTS. Nhiều phiên chat Codex/Claude nhận job riêng cùng worker CLI/API độc lập; quota nền không giới hạn phiên chat. Hoạt động theo dõi chung; không thêm sidebar/editor trùng. Mỗi máy chạy local, NAS chia sẻ nguồn; không bổ sung scheduler liên máy.

Code giao lô hiện có: contentos-batch-edit.js + batch-edit-dispatch.mjs + stageFirstBuild dùng chung trong server. Sau nhập mỗi video thành một dự án, người dùng giao mẫu/execution/completion chung và brief riêng; journal/marker chống lặp cùng khóa, lỗi giữ phần đã giao. Có điểm mở từ Home và lô nhập thành công trong Hoạt động. Điều này cập nhật giới hạn cũ ở phần nhập nguồn bên dưới: intake vẫn không tự gọi AI, bước giao AI là thao tác riêng.

Bằng chứng đã lưu từ mốc code trước lần sửa tài liệu: 190 service tests đạt(temp D); INTAKE_BATCH_API_SMOKE.json kiểm hai project thật fixture, brief/candidate riêng, retry không đổi job và hai inbox session claim hai job; review không render, không gọi model. Chưa kiểm dialog giao AI bằng browser, partial/crash journal đầy đủ, hồi quy first-build sau refactor, triển khai main5180 hoặc chạy hỗn hợp bằng AI thật. Các kiểm browser trước chỉ bao phủ dialog nhập nguồn. Không suy ra năng suất production từ smoke.

Lần cập nhật này chỉ sửa tài liệu, không chạy lại test ứng dụng, không restart server hoặc giao việc AI. Toàn bộ tích hợp ba app còn tiếp tục; tài liệu không xác nhận full build hoàn tất.

## Completion policy and automatic final export for staged chat revisions

- Shared Space/Studio chat now has independent Sau khi AI dựng xong selector: auto-export or review, plus save-as-workspace-default. Service persists workspace defaults, resolves run > project > workspace and defaults new policy to auto-export. Job captures normalized mode/final quality/publish=false at submission; layout/agent changes do not alter its policy. Existing legacy jobs without policy continue as review.
- After staged revise commit/build succeeds, manual policy stops at awaiting-review. Auto policy launches existing snapshot/lock-based export CLI with the exact applied revision; a changed project rejects rather than silently exporting a newer edit. Completed job is stored before launch, so duplicate completion returns 409 rather than launching twice. History shows export/request/review/error states; chat completion tells the user where to observe export. No publication is triggered.
- Twenty-three service tests pass, including precedence, persistence, invalid policy and publish separation. Isolated API smoke applied synthetic candidates in both modes: manual produced no render, auto created a real final MP4 at captured revision, duplicate completion rejected and exactly one snapshot exists: COMPLETION_EXPORT_API_SMOKE.json. Existing staged revision/source CLI smoke passed with explicit review policy.
- Limits: only staged chat revise completion wired; Home first-build jobs not migrated. A crash between stored completion and launch is not recovered; failed/busy export requires explicit retry through export UI, no durable completion queue yet. Model/AI quality was not tested; candidates were synthetic, no provider calls. Central settings screen/project override controls, complete export observation/recovery and full product integration remain unfinished.

## Cross-process render exclusion for editor and AI CLI

- Both entry points now acquire the same atomic directory lock under workspace/contentos/render-lock before snapshot preparation or status writes. Lock records process/project/kind and renderer child PID. A competing process rejects with busy instead of overwriting render.json or launching another render. Server retains the lock through postprocessing; CLI releases on its terminal process exit after renderer close.
- Preparation failure/cancel and spawn failure release ownership. The CLI now starts a fresh cancellation scope so a completed cancelled run does not automatically cancel the next export. Uncertain/incomplete lock records and dead-owner locks remain blocked; no timeout-based takeover that could start a second orphan renderer.
- Twenty-two service tests pass including a real competing Node process, owner/child metadata, idempotent release and orphan/initialization rejection. Real API render smoke rejected a competing AI CLI without changing its pre-existing status file, then completed the original render: RENDER_CONTEXT_API_SMOKE.json. No paid AI or publishing used.
- Remaining: verified stale-lock recovery with child-process identity, cancellation during preparation/postprocessing, durable queue/worker recovery, policy-driven auto-export and full integration. Lock is per ContentOS installation; it does not coordinate the preserved original apps or other installations.

## AI export CLI migrated to shared snapshots and artifact finalization

- editor/tools/xuat-video.mjs now captures project/HTML/referenced local assets through the same createRenderSnapshot service as direct editor render. It runs HyperFrames in the snapshot folder and keeps previous project outputs. Preparation failures report terminal error instead of ignoring failed build; cancellation observed after preparation prevents spawning render. Destination is captured at start rather than read after render.
- Both paths now call render-artifacts.mjs for local loudness normalization, positive video probe, exact project dimensions and duration within tolerance, non-overwriting output and snapshot/revision sidecar. Snapshot manifest retains external dependency URLs as before. A failed destination copy reports saveError while preserving the verified local result.
- Twenty-one service tests pass, including actual synthetic MP4 finalization and rejection of mismatched dimensions/overwrite. Actual AI export CLI produced a verified three-second H.264/AAC output with snapshot/revision metadata: AI_EXPORT_SNAPSHOT_SMOKE.json. Direct API snapshot/context render passed again after sharing finalization: RENDER_CONTEXT_API_SMOKE.json. No AI provider or publication invoked.
- Remaining: mutual exclusion across server/CLI processes, durable queue ownership/leases, restart recovery, cancellation during postprocessing, snapshot retention, automated completion/export policy, production-scale validation and full feature integration. This migrates the export command, not automatic AI completion.

## Local render snapshots and output lineage

- Direct editor render now builds a captured project revision and copies referenced local composition files into work/render-snapshots/<uuid>. Each copy has checksum/size in snapshot.json; unused media is not copied and no hard links are used. Checks source file size/mtime during transfer and revision again after preparation; failures remove the partial snapshot. Disk reserve and file-count limits apply. Local references escaping the project or missing files fail preparation.
- HyperFrames runs against snapshot composition/assets, then publishes a separate non-overwriting project output. Result/adjacent output metadata records snapshot ID, project revision, probe and terminal outcome. Cancel during preparation prevents spawning render after copying finishes. Snapshot retention/pruning and progress/cancellation mid-copy remain unfinished.
- Twenty service tests pass, including asset independence after source replacement, frozen project JSON, unused-file exclusion, stale revision/missing media/escape rejection and cleanup. Real API render test changed live index.html to deliberately invalid content while rendering from the snapshot, switched projects, and verified successful original output. Evidence RENDER_CONTEXT_API_SMOKE.json. Only synthetic media used.
- Boundaries: external GSAP/font URLs are recorded, not frozen or vendored; source changes preserving size/mtime are not fully detected. Snapshots capture referenced composition assets, not arbitrary files loaded dynamically by user code. AI CLI export path is not yet migrated; durable execution/restart, complete UI history, auto-export, production benchmarks and full integration remain outstanding.

## Workspace image connection UI and Windows secret storage

- Added shared Kết nối ảnh dialog reachable from the existing image tool in library/editor/cover. Saves gateway base URL/model and optional API key for the selected workspace without calling a provider. Server status never returns plaintext/ciphertext keys. Changing endpoint clears retained credentials unless a new key is explicitly entered. Save/delete pin the workspace observed when the dialog loaded and reject stale workspace selection.
- Connection configuration is stored outside project/NAS/app media under LOCALAPPDATA/ContentOS/connections/<hash-of-app-root>/<workspace>.json. API key uses Windows current-user DPAPI via PowerShell secure-string conversion; secret is passed through stdin, not command arguments. PowerShell child clears inherited PSModulePath to avoid incompatible PowerShell 7 module lookup. DPAPI failure fails closed without saving plaintext. Other platforms currently require environment-based secrets.
- Image service uses a saved workspace connection when present, otherwise existing CONTENTOS_IMAGE_* environment fallback. It snapshots config at generation start. Removing the workspace override restores environment fallback. This is initial image-only connection infrastructure, not yet the unified LLM/voice/social secret store, remote access control or key rotation service.
- Sixteen service tests passed including real Windows DPAPI roundtrip, encrypted file content, hidden API key status, workspace separation and endpoint-change credential clearing. Isolated synthetic image API smoke now saves workspace configuration and uses it for mock generation→asset→attachment→exact export; no real provider/credential used: `IMAGE_API_SMOKE.json`. No production keys copied or displayed.
- Chrome verified the shared image-connection dialog and empty password field from the editor; existing cover/layout/job recovery checks passed with no page errors: `SPACE_STUDIO_BROWSER_SMOKE.json`. Full browser form save remains covered by service/API checks rather than a browser submission; no claim of live provider validity.

## Revise job recovery and project history

- `/api/ai-revise/history` loads stored revise jobs scoped to the guarded editor project, returns latest 30 plus active waiting/working job. Shared chat restores observation after page reload, disables new sends while observing and resumes existing result handling. No new agent or job is launched by recovery itself. Working status is persisted evidence of claim, not worker liveness; lease-based VEK execution remains outstanding.
- New revise requests reject active project work before and after async source inspection, preventing two windows from creating concurrent revise jobs through this route. New staged revise cancellation retains a cancelled record and candidate, invalidates completion/import, and UI reports cancellation only after server acknowledgement. History dialog shows instructions, status and summary/error. Retrying staged jobs checks the captured base revision.
- Thirteen service tests passed. Runtime API smoke verifies scoped active history and AI_JOB_ACTIVE rejection. Chrome smoke sent a synthetic session-mode job (no AI provider), reloaded, verified disabled Send and recovered cancel control, cancelled it, then observed Đã hủy in history; no page errors. Evidence: `SPACE_STUDIO_BROWSER_SMOKE.json`, `REVISION_RESULT_API_SMOKE.json`.
- Limitations: server scans local revise job metadata; no server-backed conversation store, history pagination/retention or durable queue migration. A permanently orphaned working record requires cancellation; recovery does not prove the external worker lives or restart it. Completed jobs are visible in history, but terminal-notification dedup and cross-device chat sync are not implemented. Full production scope remains incomplete.

## Explicit CLI model override

- Shared chat now has optional Model CLI input only for separate CLI execution. Blank uses CLI configuration. Normalization preserves supplied model IDs, rejects malformed/control/shell-like input and rejects model overrides in active-session mode. No model names or account availability are invented.
- Runner checks installed executable help for --model support before adding the requested ID as its own argv value. Codex uses exec --model; Claude gets --model only if its installed help confirms support. Unsupported capability fails the job instead of silently ignoring the selection. Requested model is recorded in runner log; this is not proof of the model actually served.
- Installed `codex exec --help` was read locally and advertises -m/--model. No Claude executable was available in the usual PATH/local-bin locations inspected; Claude runtime capability is therefore unverified and gated at execution. Twelve service tests passed, including model input validation and capability gating. Chrome smoke verified CLI model draft restoration with existing layout/source/intake regression: `SPACE_STUDIO_BROWSER_SMOKE.json`.
- No real CLI agent or provider request was launched. Accepted model catalog, account support, automatic budget/capability routing, usage metering and API adapters remain unfinished. This implements explicit CLI model request plumbing, not complete model routing.

## Isolated AI revise result integration

- New requests through the existing VEK Nhờ AI sửa route capture base-project.json and candidate-project.json in a unique job directory. Inbox instructions target the candidate, preserve media paths relative to the original project, and prohibit editing/building the original during AI work. Legacy jobs without a candidate retain their original flow.
- `ai-inbox --done` for candidate jobs calls `/api/ai-revise/complete` on ContentOS port 5180. The server validates job status and base revision, checks candidate project/source structure, generates composition in the job directory, then commits with history/revision checking and builds the live preview. A newer manual revision causes HTTP 409; candidate and live project remain available. Repeated completion is rejected. Build validation is structural; it does not prove media readability or final render quality.
- Final live-build failure after commit records error plus appliedRevision so the job does not look unapplied. This milestone still has no interprocess project-file lock or transactional filesystem/database operation.
- Seven service tests passed. Runtime smoke created only synthetic footage/project, exercised actual inbox CLI completion, confirmed changed title in live project, duplicate completion rejection, and newer manual edit/candidate preservation. Evidence: `REVISION_RESULT_API_SMOKE.json`. No AI provider/render/post executed. Pending conflict QA job was marked terminal error after verification.
- Remaining: connect VEK job lifecycle to durable task leases/checkpoints, recover/apply conflicted results in UI, stage first-build AI jobs and their generated assets, Space/Studio, NAS chat, model adapters and export policy. Task queue is still not the executor for this path.

## Persisted task foundation and revision safeguards

- Added local task store `src/services/tasks.mjs` and `/api/v1/tasks` list/create/get/cancel/retry endpoints. Creation captures stored project and SHA256 revision, validates editor identity/revision and active workspace, and requires an idempotency key. Repeated keys return the existing task only for identical requests.
- Internal worker lifecycle supports exclusive claim, renewable 60-second lease, checkpoint, finish/failure and retry. Expired running work becomes interrupted on the next store access, never silently rerun. Stale workers cannot finish cancelled/retried tasks. Atomic queue file: workspace/contentos/tasks/queue.json. One local server owns the store; concurrent direct file writers are unsupported.
- This is a foundation API. Existing VEK inbox jobs are not migrated; no new queue worker, UI, result application, artifact validation or auto-export is wired. Checkpoints do not yet resume AI execution. Task success alone does not prove video production.
- Guarded save/build paths validate project identity/revision; saves preserve history and use atomic replacement. File hashing detects external changes. API smoke separately proves revocation. In-app browser control was unavailable during this pass; REA was used for UI verification.

## Khóa dự án và quyền hoàn tất của phiên nhận việc · 04/11/2026

session-job-claims thêm reserveProjectJob dùng work/.ai-execution.lock theo real project, cùng cho inbox session/CLI và startApiEditor. Hai file job nhắm một project chỉ một nhận; project khác không bị khóa theo. Matching terminal owner (done/error/cancelled + đúng claim ID) có thể được thay dưới inspection gate; owner mất/đổi/hỏng/không rõ không tự cướp lock. API bận trả running:false/busy, giữ waiting và chưa gọi provider; scheduler tự nhận lại vẫn chưa có. Crash giữa reserve và write giữ trạng thái cần kiểm tra, chưa recovery UI.

Inbox in mã --claim và thêm vào lệnh hướng dẫn --buoc/--done/--fail; kiểm owner trước ghi progress/candidate hoặc báo done/fail. HTTP build/revise completion kiểm claimId; build recheck sau async import. Job cũ không claimedBy giữ tương thích. Không coi claim ID là security boundary với process đọc được cùng workspace; đây là chống nhầm và trùng thực thi. Chưa enforce mọi tool viết candidate, job replacement/cancel/runner exit ownership hoặc mọi route xóa/ghi lịch sử. Khóa chưa phải scheduler/heartbeat registry; ASR/render chưa điều phối nhiều nguồn.

## Điều phối worker nền giới hạn song song · 04/11/2026

background-dispatch.mjs giữ FIFO các yêu cầu khởi chạy trong process, giới hạn1–8 worker (mặc định2), bỏ qua dự án bận để việc độc lập tiến lên, timer nhận việc kế khi maps runner/API có chỗ. startAgentRunner/startApiEditor đưa yêu cầu vào cùng dispatcher, pinned job identity được kiểm lại trước launch; session inbox độc lập không chiếm quota worker nền. stopAgentRunner bỏ pending entry. API validate bổ sung claim identity; setJobError không ghi đè cancelled. CLI fallback nội bộ giữ luồng hiện có, chưa kiểm real CLI lifecycle/replacement.

GET/POST /api/v1/background-dispatch, settings local chung máy với workspace guard khi save; Cài đặt có form Dựng song song trên máy này (max worker + ngừng nhận thêm). Activity đọc số active/quota/pending lúc mở; chưa live-refresh riêng summary worker. Pause không hủy việc đang chạy. ASR/render chưa có semaphore mới. Pending chưa persist và không tự phục hồi sau restart; API startup vẫn đánh dấu gián đoạn, không tự replay provider. Không coi danh sách queue persisted taskStore là dispatcher này.

Bổ sung person_mask_guard.py kiểm giữa hai mẫu: overlap nhỏ hơn 5% diện tích vùng nhỏ và tâm di chuyển trên 20% tọa độ khung chuẩn hóa, hoặc tỷ lệ diện tích trên 4 lần thì từ chối toàn kết quả của lượt. Đây là heuristic liên tục, không xác minh danh tính. Chuyển động nhanh/ra khỏi khung có thể bị từ chối nhầm; nhiều người chồng nhau có thể không bị phát hiện. Người dùng vẫn cần xem mask toàn khoảng. Không suy đã phục hồi che khuất/cắt cảnh.

# Hoàn tất bản xuất không chặn server · phân tích trước triển khai

Hiện server close handler gọi đồng bộ FFmpeg loudnorm, ffprobe, đo audio/hash/copy. Video dài có thể chặn mọi request dù renderer đã thoát. Nhiều phiên dựng cần API/Hoạt động vẫn phản hồi. Tách finalizer hiện có vào worker thread Node, server await nhưng event loop không bị chặn; giữ cùng thuật toán/artifact/lock và không nhân pipeline.

Trong lúc hoàn tất: running=true/finalizing=true, ok chưa chốt, khóa render vẫn giữ tới kết quả/metadata. UI ghi Đang hoàn tất file và kiểm âm thanh, không giả progress mới. Hủy khi renderer đã thoát không thể đảo commit: API trả409 rõ giai đoạn, UI ẩn nút Hủy. Không tự terminate thread đang commit. Tham khảo component VEK renderRenderBox đã có và ảnh1280/390 RENDER_FINAL_AUDIO; không thêm menu. CLI chạy process riêng giữ finalizer đồng bộ; main server dùng asyncworker. Kiểm heartbeat event loop cùng FFmpeg thật/sidecar/hash, HTTP status và error, UI component. Không đổi renderer/composition/skill.


## Kết quả

Đã thêm render-finalization-async.mjs và worker entry dùng lại finalizeRenderArtifact nguyên thuật toán. Main close callback await worker exit rồi chốt result, trong lúc đó runningtrue/finalizingtrue/oknull, giữ lock render. Worker lỗi có phản hồi/exitcheck, không resolve trước khi thread thoát. CLI xuat-video chạy riêng vẫn dùng finalizer sync, không đổi hành vi. Hash/copy/FFmpeg đều ở thread của finalizer thay mainloop; renderer vẫn chạy process hiện có.

UI báo đang hoàn tất file/kiểm âm thanh, ẩn Hủy trong giai đoạn này; DELETE render-ai trả409 rõ để tránh hủy giữa commit. Không tự terminate thread hoặc công bố render xong trước verify. Chưa có crash recovery cho thread khi cả app chết, giữ giới hạn hiện hành.
